   1++6C16              ; ============================================================================
   2++6C16              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6C16              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6C16              ;
   5++6C16              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6C16              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6C16              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6C16              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6C16              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6C16              ; ============================================================================
  11++6C16              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6C16              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6C16              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6C16              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6C16              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6C16              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6C16
  18++6C16              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6C16                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6C16 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6C19 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6C1C B7                   or      a
  23++6C1D ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6C1F C2 5E 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6C22
  26++6C22                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6C22 21 44 86             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6C25 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6C28 B7                   or      a
  30++6C29 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6C2B DA 5E 6C             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6C2E CA 5E 6C             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6C31 22 78 6C             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6C34 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6C35 FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6C37 D2 5E 6C             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6C3A
  38++6C3A 21 44 86             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6C3D 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6C40 B7                   or      a
  41++6C41 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6C43 DA 4C 6C             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6C46 CA 4C 6C             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6C49 C3 5E 6C             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6C4C              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6C4C                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6C4C 3A 5B 73             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6C4F FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6C51 C2 5E 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6C54 3A 58 73             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6C57 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6C59 C2 5E 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6C5C B7                   or      a
  54++6C5D C9                   ret
  55++6C5E
  56++6C5E              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6C5E 37                   scf
  58++6C5F C9                   ret
  59++6C60
  60++6C60              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6C60              p127_layout_report:
  62++6C60 21 7A 6C             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size
  63++6C63 CD 98 62             call    p127_puts
  64++6C66 3A 79 6C             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size+1)

  67++6C6F CD B4 62             call    p127_put_hex8
  68++6C72 21 96 6C             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size_tail
  69++6C75 C3 98 62             jp      p127_puts
  70++6C78
  71++6C78              p127_kernel_size:
  72++6C78 00 00                dw      0
  73++6C7A              p127_msg_kernel_size:

  74++6C7E 37 5D 20 4B

 474+ 6CB2              ; Keep the remap-critical code/data at the start of the Stage2 image so it
 475+ 6CB2              ; remains inside W1/P5. The preflight below verifies the actual linked bounds.
 476+ 6CB2                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++6CB2              ; ============================================================================
   2++6CB2              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6CB2              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6CB2              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6CB2              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6CB2              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6CB2              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6CB2              ; ============================================================================
   9++6CB2              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6CB2              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6CB2              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6CB2
  13++6CB2              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6CB2              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6CB2              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6CB2              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6CB2              p127_resident_start:
  18++6CB2
  19++6CB2              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6CB2              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6CB2              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6CB2              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6CB2              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6CB2              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6CB2
  26++6CB2              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6CB2              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6CB2              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6CB2              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6CB2              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6CB2 32 3C 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6CB5 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6CB6 32 3D 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6CB9 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6CBA 32 3E 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6CBD 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6CBE 32 3F 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6CC1
  39++6CC1 3A 3C 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6CC4 FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6CC6 C2 F0 6C             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6CC9 3A 3D 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6CCC FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6CCE C2 F0 6C             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6CD1 3A 3E 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6CD4 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6CD6 C2 F0 6C             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6CD9 3A 3F 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6CDC E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6CDE CA F0 6C             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6CE1                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6CE1 3A 3C 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6CE4 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6CE6 CA F0 6C             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6CE9 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6CEB CA F0 6C             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6CEE B7                   or      a
  58++6CEF C9                   ret
  59++6CF0
  60++6CF0              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6CF0 37                   scf
  62++6CF1 C9                   ret
  63++6CF2
  64++6CF2              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6CF2              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6CF2              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++6CF2 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++6CF4 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++6CF6 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++6CF8 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++6CFA CD B2 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++6CFD DA 3A 6D             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++6D00
  74++6D00 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++6D02 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++6D04 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++6D06 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++6D08 CD B2 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++6D0B D2 3A 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++6D0E
  81++6D0E 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++6D10 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++6D12 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++6D14 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++6D16 CD B2 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++6D19 D2 3A 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++6D1C
  88++6D1C 3E 03                ld      a,3
  89++6D1E 06 01                ld      b,1
  90++6D20 0E 02                ld      c,2
  91++6D22 16 00                ld      d,0
  92++6D24 CD B2 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  93++6D27 D2 3A 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  94++6D2A

  96++6D2C 06 00                ld      b,0
  97++6D2E 0E 02                ld      c,2
  98++6D30 16 01                ld      d,1
  99++6D32 CD B2 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
 100++6D35 D2 3A 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
 101++6D38 B7                   or      a
 102++6D39 C9                   ret

 248++6E24              ; Save/restore the complete software W3 ownership tuple.
 249++6E24              ; Snapshot and rollback are RAM-only; they never touch page registers.

 270++6E4C              ; Recovery tests: reject a competing acquire, preserve a busy UI when
 271++6E4C              ; quiesce is refused, roll back a partial HANDOFF acquire, reject a
 272++6E4C              ; transition from HANDOFF, then resume and prove UI admission works again.
 273++6E4C              p127_w3_recovery_selftest:

 349++6EE3              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++6EE3              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++6EE3              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++6EE3              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++6EE3              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++6EE3              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++6EE3              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.

   1++6F32              ; ============================================================================
   2++6F32              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++6F32              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++6F32              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++6F32              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++6F32              ; ============================================================================
   7++6F32              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++6F32              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++6F32              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++6F32              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++6F32              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++6F32              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++6F32
  14++6F32              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++6F32              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++6F32              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++6F32              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++6F32              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++6F32              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++6F32              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++6F32              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++6F32              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++6F32              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++6F32              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++6F32
  26++6F32              p127_irq_init:
  27++6F32 F3                   di
  28++6F33 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++6F35 32 14 72             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++6F38 AF                   xor     a
  31++6F39 32 15 72             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++6F3C 32 16 72             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++6F3F 32 17 72             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++6F42 B7                   or      a
  35++6F43 C9                   ret
  36++6F44

  38++6F44              p127_irq_validate:

   1++7218              ; ============================================================================
   2++7218              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++7218              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4++7218              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++7218              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++7218              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++7218              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++7218              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++7218              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++7218              ; ============================================================================
  11++7218              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++7218              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  13++7218              P127_MEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3

  16++7218              P127_MEM_FREE          EQU 0

   1++7396              ; ============================================================================
   2++7396              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++7396              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++7396              ; ABI:
   5++7396              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++7396              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++7396              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++7396              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++7396              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++7396              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.
  11++7396              ; ============================================================================
  12++7396              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  13++7396              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  14++7396              P127_RANGE_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  15++7396              P127_RANGE_LIMIT         EQU 0100000h
  16++7396
  17++7396              p127_range_validate:
  18++7396                      ; Add the unsigned 32-bit length to the unsigned start.
  19++7396 21 A4 74             ld      hl,p127_range_start
  20++7399 11 A8 74             ld      de,p127_range_length
  21++739C 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  22++739D 47                   ld      b,a
  23++739E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  24++739F 80                   add     a,b
  25++73A0 32 AC 74             ld      (p127_range_end),a
  26++73A3 23                   inc     hl
  27++73A4 13                   inc     de
  28++73A5 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  29++73A6 47                   ld      b,a
  30++73A7 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  31++73A8 88                   adc     a,b
  32++73A9 32 AD 74             ld      (p127_range_end+1),a
  33++73AC 23                   inc     hl
  34++73AD 13                   inc     de
  35++73AE 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  36++73AF 47                   ld      b,a
  37++73B0 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  38++73B1 88                   adc     a,b
  39++73B2 32 AE 74             ld      (p127_range_end+2),a
  40++73B5 23                   inc     hl
  41++73B6 13                   inc     de
  42++73B7 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  43++73B8 47                   ld      b,a
  44++73B9 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  45++73BA 88                   adc     a,b
  46++73BB 32 AF 74             ld      (p127_range_end+3),a
  47++73BE D8                   ret     c                       ; unsigned 32-bit addition overflow
  48++73BF
  49++73BF                      ; End-exclusive address must be <= 0x00100000.
  50++73BF 3A AF 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+3)
  51++73C2 B7                   or      a
  52++73C3 C2 E1 73             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  53++73C6 3A AE 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+2)
  54++73C9 FE 10                cp      010h
  55++73CB DA DF 73             jp      c,p127_range_valid
  56++73CE C2 E1 73             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  57++73D1 3A AD 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+1)
  58++73D4 B7                   or      a
  59++73D5 C2 E1 73             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  60++73D8 3A AC 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end)
  61++73DB B7                   or      a
  62++73DC C2 E1 73             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  63++73DF              p127_range_valid:
  64++73DF B7                   or      a
  65++73E0 C9                   ret
  66++73E1              p127_range_invalid:
  67++73E1 37                   scf
  68++73E2 C9                   ret
  69++73E3
  70++73E3              ; Regression tests exercise the 1 MiB boundary, zero length and uint32 wrap.
  71++73E3              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failed assertion.
  72++73E3              p127_range_selftest:
  73++73E3                      ; [0, 0) is valid.
  74++73E3 AF                   xor     a
  75++73E4 21 A4 74             ld      hl,p127_range_start
  76++73E7 77                   ld      (hl),a
  77++73E8 23                   inc     hl
  78++73E9 77                   ld      (hl),a
  79++73EA 23                   inc     hl

  87++73F4 23                   inc     hl
  88++73F5 77                   ld      (hl),a
  89++73F6 23                   inc     hl
  90++73F7 77                   ld      (hl),a
  91++73F8 CD 96 73             call    p127_range_validate
  92++73FB DA A2 74             jp      c,p127_range_test_fail
  93++73FE

   1++74B0              ; ============================================================================
   2++74B0              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++74B0              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++74B0              ;
   5++74B0              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++74B0              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++74B0              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++74B0              ;
   9++74B0              ; API:
  10++74B0              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++74B0              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++74B0              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++74B0              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++74B0              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++74B0              ;
  16++74B0              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++74B0              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++74B0              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++74B0              ; ============================================================================
  20++74B0              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++74B0              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++74B0              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++74B0              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++74B0              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++74B0
  26++74B0              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++74B0              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++74B0              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++74B0              p127_arena_init:
  30++74B0 21 95 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1

   1++760E              ; ============================================================================
   2++760E              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++760E              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++760E              ;
   5++760E              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++760E              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++760E              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++760E              ;
   9++760E              ; API:
  10++760E              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++760E              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++760E              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++760E              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++760E              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++760E              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++760E              ; ============================================================================
  17++760E              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++760E              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++760E              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  20++760E              P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT EQU 256
  21++760E              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++760E              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh
  23++760E
  24++760E              p127_as_init:
  25++760E 21 AF 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  26++7611 36 FF                ld      (hl),P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  27++7613 11 B0 77             ld      de,p127_as_page_table+1
  28++7616 01 FF 00             ld      bc,P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT-1
  29++7619 ED B0                ldir
  30++761B B7                   or      a
  31++761C C9                   ret
  32++761D
  33++761D              ; Bind an allocator-owned physical page. Physical aliases are forbidden.
  34++761D              p127_as_bind:

  40++7629 5F                   ld      e,a

  42++762C 21 56 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
  43++762F 19                   add     hl,de
  44++7630 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  45++7631 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
  46++7633 C2 C1 76             jp      nz,p127_as_reject
  47++7636
  48++7636 3A AA 77             ld      a,(p127_as_logical_temp)
  49++7639 5F                   ld      e,a
  50++763A 16 00                ld      d,0

  55++7643 C2 C1 76             jp      nz,p127_as_reject
  56++7646

  67++7658              .p127_as_bind_next:

  69++7659 10 F0                djnz    .p127_as_bind_scan
  70++765B

  82++766B              p127_as_unbind:

  95++767C              ; Page index = address >> 14; offset = address & 3FFFh.
  96++767C              p127_as_translate:

   1++7A8F              ; ============================================================================
   2++7A8F              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7A8F              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7A8F              ;
   5++7A8F              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7A8F              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7A8F              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7A8F              ;
   9++7A8F              ; API inputs:
  10++7A8F              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7A8F              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7A8F              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7A8F              ; Outputs:
  14++7A8F              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7A8F              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7A8F              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7A8F              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7A8F              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7A8F              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7A8F              ; ============================================================================

  22++7A8F              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1

   2++8011              ; Project127 ATM Core — pure software page-mapper model

   6++8011              P127_MAP_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0

  13++8011              ; Shadow map reflects the verified bootstrap layout:
  14++8011              ; W0=TR-DOS ROM, W1=RAM P5, W2=RAM P2, W3=RAM P0.
  15++8011              ; This table is descriptive state only; it does not program hardware.
  16++8011              p127_map_init:

   1++8333              ; ============================================================================
   2++8333              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++8333              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8333              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++8333              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.
   6++8333              ; ============================================================================
   7++8333              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++8333              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
   9++8333              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++8333
  11++8333              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++8333              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++8333              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++8333              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++8333              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++8333              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++8333              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++8333
  19++8333              ; IN: none.
  20++8333              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++8333              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++8333              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++8333              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++8333              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++8333              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++8333              p127_handoff_stack_probe:
  27++8333 F3                   di
  28++8334 F5                   push    af
  29++8335 C5                   push    bc
  30++8336 D5                   push    de
  31++8337 E5                   push    hl
  32++8338 DD E5                push    ix
  33++833A FD E5                push    iy
  34++833C ED 73 CB 83          ld      (p127_handoff_saved_sp),sp
  35++8340
  36++8340                      ; Refuse if the live stack has crossed its declared low-water limit.
  37++8340 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
  38++8343 39                   add     hl,sp
  39++8344 11 00 B9             ld      de,P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP
  40++8347 B7                   or      a
  41++8348 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++834A DA 9B 83             jp      c,p127_handoff_stack_refuse

   1++83CE              ; ============================================================================
   2++83CE              ; Project127 TR-DOS directory reader
   3++83CE              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++83CE              ; Reads track 0 sectors 1..8 via the documented TR-DOS ROM entry #3D13.
   5++83CE              ; Directory snapshot: 128 * 16 bytes at #C800 (reserved host/staging page P0).
   6++83CE              ; Read-only: no file-data reads, writes, DMA, IM/EI or raw paging OUT.
   7++83CE              ; ============================================================================

   9++83CE              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1

  14++83CE              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_SIZE EQU 16
  15++83CE

   1+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A000              ; Project127 ATM-native debug console, ported from Project126
   3+ A000              ; Source: Project126/atm_host/p126_screen_console.asm
   4+ A000              ; Module version: 0.1.3
   5+ A000              ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG6 80x25; upper 12 rows status, lower 13 rows scrolling log.
   6+ A000              ; Every displayed debug character and static UI label is mirrored to #EF.
   7+ A000              ; Resident placement: #A000, in the Project126 host display window.

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret

 239+ A149              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.

 247+ A150 DA B9 A1             jp      c,.p126_print_at_denied
 248+ A153                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 249+ A153                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.

 251+ A154 D1                   pop     de

 262+ A165              .p126_print_at_loop:
 263+ A165 2A 0D A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_text_ptr)

 267+ A16D 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a

  93  A3F2                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_physmem.asm"
# file opened: p127_physmem.asm
   1+ A3F2              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A3F2              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A3F2              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A3F2              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A3F2              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A3F2              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A3F2              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A3F2
  15+ A3F2              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A3F2              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A3F2              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A3F2              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A3F2              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A3F2              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A3F2              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:
  22+ A3F2 3E 53                ld      a,'S'
  23+ A3F4 CD A0 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
  24+ A3F7 AF                   xor     a
  25+ A3F8 32 63 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
  26+ A3FB 32 64 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  27+ A3FE 32 65 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_quiesced),a
  28+ A401 32 66 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  29+ A404 32 67 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a
  30+ A407 32 68 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  31+ A40A
  32+ A40A                      ; The host/staging page must be the documented W3 page before entry.
  33+ A40A 3A 0A 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
  34+ A40D B7                   or      a
  35+ A40E C2 A5 A6             jp      nz,p127_physmem_fail_map_state
  36+ A411
  37+ A411 CD 95 72             call    p127_mem_alloc
  38+ A414 DA AA A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_alloc
  39+ A417 32 69 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_a),a

  48+ A42E 3E 02                ld      a,2
  49+ A430 32 64 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  50+ A433
  51+ A433 3A 69 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)

  54+ A43A B8                   cp      b

  56+ A43E
  57+ A43E                      ; No UI routine may run while W3 is reassigned.
  58+ A43E CD 70 6D             call    p127_w3_quiesce
  59+ A441 DA AF A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_lease

  63+ A44C DA AF A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_lease
  64+ A44F 3E 01                ld      a,1
  65+ A451 32 66 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  66+ A454
  67+ A454                      ; Preserve one byte from P0 for a non-destructive port-open probe.
  68+ A454 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
  69+ A457 32 6C AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_p0),a
  70+ A45A 2F                   cpl
  71+ A45B 32 6D AB             ld      (p127_physmem_probe),a
  72+ A45E 3E 01                ld      a,1
  73+ A460 32 68 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  74+ A463
  75+ A463                      ; Select allocated page A and write one complemented probe byte.
  76+ A463 3A 69 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
  77+ A466 CD 26 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
  78+ A469 DA B4 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
  79+ A46C 3E 01                ld      a,1
  80+ A46E 32 67 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a
  81+ A471 3A 6D AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_probe)
  82+ A474 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
  83+ A477
  84+ A477                      ; Return to P0. Its saved byte must be unchanged if the OUT worked.
  85+ A477 AF                   xor     a
  86+ A478 CD 26 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
  87+ A47B DA B4 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map

 224+ A5DE                      ; Stage 2.19: exhaust the allocator into a private page-ID list.

 226+ A5DE                      ; checked against all prior IDs before it is appended.

 452+ A76A              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A76A              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A76A              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:

 456+ A76A                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.

 458+ A76A                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.

 576+ A8A6              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ A8A6              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.

 585+ A8B0 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ A8B2 FE 07                cp      7
 587+ A8B4 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ A8B6 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ A8B8 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ A8BA FE 01                cp      1
 591+ A8BC 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ A8BE FE 02                cp      2
 593+ A8C0 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ A8C2 FE 03                cp      3
 595+ A8C4 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ A8C6 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ A8C8 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ A8CA FE 05                cp      5
 599+ A8CC 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ A8CE 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ A8D0              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ A8D0 C5                   push    bc
 603+ A8D1 E5                   push    hl
 604+ A8D2 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ A8D3 16 00                ld      d,0
 606+ A8D5 21 56 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 607+ A8D8 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ A8D9 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 609+ A8DA FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 610+ A8DC E1                   pop     hl
 611+ A8DD C1                   pop     bc
 612+ A8DE 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ A8E0              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:
 614+ A8E0 0C                   inc     c
 615+ A8E1 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ A8E3 B7                   or      a
 617+ A8E4 C9                   ret
 618+ A8E5              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ A8E5 37                   scf
 620+ A8E6 C9                   ret
 621+ A8E7
 622+ A8E7              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ A8E7              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ A8E7 3A 64 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ A8EA 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ A8EB 21 71 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ A8EE              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:
 628+ A8EE 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 629+ A8EF 5F                   ld      e,a
 630+ A8F0 16 00                ld      d,0
 631+ A8F2 C5                   push    bc
 632+ A8F3 E5                   push    hl
 633+ A8F4 21 56 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 634+ A8F7 19                   add     hl,de
 635+ A8F8 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 636+ A8F9 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 637+ A8FB E1                   pop     hl
 638+ A8FC C1                   pop     bc
 639+ A8FD 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail
 640+ A8FF 23                   inc     hl
 641+ A900 10 EC                djnz    p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop
 642+ A902 B7                   or      a
 643+ A903 C9                   ret
 644+ A904              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ A904 37                   scf
 646+ A905 C9                   ret
 647+ A906
 648+ A906              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ A906              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ A906              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ A906 21 56 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ A909 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ A90B 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ A90D              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ A90D 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ A90E FE 00                cp      0
 657+ A910 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ A912 FE 01                cp      1
 659+ A914 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ A916 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ A918 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ A91A FE 03                cp      3
 663+ A91C 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ A91E FE 04                cp      4
 665+ A920 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ A922 FE 05                cp      5
 667+ A924 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ A926 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ A928 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ A92A 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ A92B B7                   or      a
 672+ A92C 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ A92E 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ A930              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ A930 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ A931 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ A933 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ A935              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ A935 23                   inc     hl
 680+ A936 0C                   inc     c
 681+ A937 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ A939 B7                   or      a
 683+ A93A C9                   ret
 684+ A93B              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ A93B 37                   scf
 686+ A93C C9                   ret
 687+ A93D
 688+ A93D              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ A93D              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ A93D
 691+ A93D              ; ============================================================================
 692+ A93D              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ A93D              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ A93D              ; Public ABI:
 695+ A93D              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ A93D              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ A93D              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ A93D              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ A93D              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ A93D              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ A93D              ; ============================================================================
 702+ A93D              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ A93D C3 95 72             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ A940
 705+ A940              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ A940 32 9D AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ A943 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ A946 B7                   or      a
 709+ A947 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ A94A 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ A94D 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ A94E 3A 0A 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ A951 B8                   cp      b
 714+ A952 CA FA A9             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ A955 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ A958 C3 AB 72             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ A95B
 718+ A95B              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ A95B              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ A95B F3                   di
 721+ A95C 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ A95F B7                   or      a
 723+ A960 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ A963 3A 0A 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ A966 B7                   or      a
 726+ A967 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ A96A CD 70 6D             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ A96D D8                   ret     c
 729+ A96E 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ A970 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ A973 CD 8F 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ A976 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ A978 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ A97A 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ A97D 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ A980 32 9C AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ A983 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ A985 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ A988 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ A98B B7                   or      a
 741+ A98C C9                   ret
 742+ A98D              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ A98D CD B7 6D             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ A990 AF                   xor     a
 745+ A991 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ A994 37                   scf
 747+ A995 C9                   ret
 748+ A996
 749+ A996              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ A996              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ A996 32 9D AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ A999 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ A99C FE 01                cp      1
 754+ A99E C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ A9A1 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ A9A4 FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ A9A6 D2 FA A9             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ A9A9 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ A9AA 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ A9AC 21 56 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ A9AF 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ A9B0 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ A9B1 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ A9B3 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ A9B6 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ A9B9 C3 26 AB             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ A9BC
 768+ A9BC              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ A9BC              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ A9BC              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ A9BC 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ A9BF FE 01                cp      1
 773+ A9C1 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ A9C4 AF                   xor     a
 775+ A9C5 CD 26 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ A9C8 D8                   ret     c
 777+ A9C9 3A 9B AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ A9CC B7                   or      a
 779+ A9CD 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ A9CF 3A 9C AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ A9D2 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ A9D5              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ A9D5 3A 9A AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ A9D8 B7                   or      a
 785+ A9D9 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ A9DB CD A3 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ A9DE D8                   ret     c
 788+ A9DF AF                   xor     a
 789+ A9E0 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ A9E3              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ A9E3 3A 99 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ A9E6 B7                   or      a
 793+ A9E7 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ A9E9 CD B7 6D             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ A9EC D8                   ret     c
 796+ A9ED AF                   xor     a
 797+ A9EE 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ A9F1              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ A9F1 AF                   xor     a
 800+ A9F2 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ A9F5 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ A9F8 B7                   or      a
 803+ A9F9 C9                   ret
 804+ A9FA
 805+ A9FA              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ A9FA 37                   scf
 807+ A9FB C9                   ret
 808+ A9FC
 809+ A9FC              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ A9FC              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ A9FC              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ A9FC AF                   xor     a
 813+ A9FD 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ AA00 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ AA03 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ AA06 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ AA09 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ AA0B CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ AA0E D2 96 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ AA11 CD 3D A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AA14 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AA17 32 9E AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AA1A 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AA1D 32 9F AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AA20 CD 5B A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AA23 DA 90 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AA26                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AA26 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AA28 CD 96 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AA2B D2 8D AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AA2E 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AA31 CD 96 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AA34 DA 8D AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AA37                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AA37 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AA3A CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AA3D D2 8D AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AA40 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AA42 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AA45 CD BC A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AA48 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AA4B 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AA4E 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AA4F 3A 9F AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AA52 B8                   cp      b
 846+ AA53 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AA56 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AA59 CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AA5C DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AA5F                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AA5F CD 3D A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AA62 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AA65 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AA66 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AA69 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AA6A C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AA6D 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AA6E CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AA71 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AA74 3A DD 6E             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AA77 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AA79 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AA7C 3A DE 6E             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AA7F FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AA81 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AA84 3A 0A 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AA87 B7                   or      a
 868+ AA88 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AA8B B7                   or      a
 870+ AA8C C9                   ret
 871+ AA8D
 872+ AA8D              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AA8D CD BC A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AA90              p127_physmem_api_test_free:
 875+ AA90 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 876+ AA93 CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 877+ AA96              p127_physmem_api_test_fail:
 878+ AA96 37                   scf
 879+ AA97 C9                   ret

 890+ AAA0              p127_physmem_trace:

 892+ AAA3 ED 79                out     (c),a
 893+ AAA5 C9                   ret
 894+ AAA6
 895+ AAA6              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AAA6              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AAA6              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AAA6              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AAA6 F5                   push    af
 900+ AAA7 C5                   push    bc
 901+ AAA8 D5                   push    de
 902+ AAA9 E5                   push    hl
 903+ AAAA FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ AAAC 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify
 905+ AAAE 21 F8 AA             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_fill_text
 906+ AAB1 18 03                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 907+ AAB3              p127_physmem_trace_page_verify:
 908+ AAB3 21 06 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_verify_text
 909+ AAB6              p127_physmem_trace_page_text:
 910+ AAB6 7E                   ld      a,(hl)

 916+ AABF E1                   pop     hl

 918+ AAC2              p127_physmem_trace_page_value:

 920+ AAC5 CD D7 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_hex8

 922+ AACA CD F2 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_char

 924+ AACF CD F2 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 925+ AAD2 E1                   pop     hl
 926+ AAD3 D1                   pop     de
 927+ AAD4 C1                   pop     bc
 928+ AAD5 F1                   pop     af
 929+ AAD6 C9                   ret
 930+ AAD7
 931+ AAD7              p127_physmem_trace_hex8:
 932+ AAD7 F5                   push    af
 933+ AAD8 E6 F0                and     0F0h
 934+ AADA 0F                   rrca
 935+ AADB 0F                   rrca
 936+ AADC 0F                   rrca

 938+ AADE CD E7 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 939+ AAE1 F1                   pop     af
 940+ AAE2 E6 0F                and     00Fh
 941+ AAE4 C3 E7 AA             jp      p127_physmem_trace_nibble

 950+ AAF2

 954+ AAF7 C9                   ret
 955+ AAF8
 956+ AAF8              p127_physmem_trace_fill_text:
 957+ AAF8 46 49 4C 4C          db      "FILL PAGE ID=",0
 957+ AAFC 20 50 41 47
 957+ AB00 45 20 49 44
 957+ AB04 3D 00
 958+ AB06              p127_physmem_trace_verify_text:
 959+ AB06 56 45 52 49          db      "VERIFY PAGE ID=",0
 959+ AB0A 46 59 20 50

 959+ AB12 49 44 3D 00
 960+ AB16              p127_physmem_trace_hex_table:
 961+ AB16 30 31 32 33          db      "0123456789ABCDEF"
 961+ AB1A 34 35 36 37
 961+ AB1E 38 39 41 42
 961+ AB22 43 44 45 46
 962+ AB26
 963+ AB26              ; IN A=physical RAM page ID. Program only W3 (#FFF7), never W0/W1/W2.
 964+ AB26              ; D6=1 selects RAM, D7=0 selects the inverse D0-D5 page ID.
 965+ AB26              p127_physmem_set_w3:
 966+ AB26 32 6B AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_arg),a
 967+ AB29 CD 27 80             call    p127_map_encode_page
 968+ AB2C D8                   ret     c
 969+ AB2D 01 F7 FF             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT
 970+ AB30 ED 79                out     (c),a
 971+ AB32 3A 6B AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_arg)
 972+ AB35 32 0A 82             ld      (p127_map_shadow+3),a
 973+ AB38 B7                   or      a
 974+ AB39 C9                   ret
 975+ AB3A
 976+ AB3A              ; IN D=pattern seed. Byte(address)=ROL(address_low) XOR address_high XOR D.
 977+ AB3A              ; The same address produces a different expected value at each seed.
 978+ AB3A              p127_physmem_fill_pattern:
 979+ AB3A 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 980+ AB3D 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 981+ AB40              .p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop:
 982+ AB40 7D                   ld      a,l
